We formulate Mental World Modeling (MWM), a generic theoretical framework that makes mental variables core components of a world model rather than posthoc rationales: MWM aintains a coupled physical-mental world state, renders a target-specific partial observation, and simulates how candidate actions jointly update both components. We instantiate the framework in MENTIS, a training-free and fully inspectable baseline that decomposes the process into state parsing, target-observation generation, action decomposition, coupled physical and mental transition, and branch-level value evaluation. On a manually constructed, quality-controlled dataset of situated decision scenarios spanning text, image, and sounding-video stories, experiments with 8 modern LLM-based world models demonstrate that explicitly modeling the mental state is essential for predicting human decisions. Deeper analyses further expose the bottlenecks of current mental world modeling. We expect MWM as a next stage of world modeling, from simulating physical scenes to simulating the minds that act in them.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Strong imposition of Dirichlet boundary velocities in structure-preserving discretizations of elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26248)
*Cristobal Ponce,Hector Ramirez,Yongxin Wu,Ning Liu,Yann Le Gorrec*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在连续层面对位移和速度场进行加性运动学分解的方法，将边界速度强加于port-Hamiltonian弹性动力学有限元模型中，从而避免使用拉格朗日乘子（产生DAE）或弱施加方法（精度差），最终得到保持ODE结构的 lifted PHS 模型，并通过数值实验验证了能量平衡和计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有port-Hamiltonian弹性动力学模型中，边界速度的施加要么依赖拉格朗日乘子导致微分代数方程（DAE），要么采用弱施加方法但在Dirichlet边界上精度较差。本文旨在克服这两类方法的局限性，发展一种能保持常微分方程（ODE）结构且边界条件强施加的高精度方法。

Method: 在连续层面对位移和速度场进行加性运动学分解：分解为在边界上消失的相对动态分量和向内域延伸的预设提升函数。该分解产生一个分布式端口，将边界驱动的影响映射到域内。通过将该映射纳入适当的虚功率原理，推导出提升的port-Hamiltonian系统（PHS）模型，经有限元离散后得到ODE系统，其中Dirichlet边界速度被强施加。该方法进一步推导出适用于不同PHS几何表示的2场和4场公式。

Result: 在特定形函数下可恢复标准有限元格式，表明离散模型中的提升框架等价于计算力学中经典的代数矩阵分区方法。数值模拟验证了该方法的能量平衡特性和计算性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的提升框架成功将边界速度强施加于port-Hamiltonian弹性动力学有限元模型，在保持ODE结构的同时避免了拉格朗日乘子的复杂性和弱施加方法的精度损失，并且与经典有限元代数分区方法等价，具有良好的计算性能。

Abstract: The imposition of boundary velocities in finite element models of port-Hamiltonian elastodynamics typically relies on Lagrange multipliers, yielding Differential-Algebraic Equations (DAEs). Alternatively, weak imposition methods that maintain an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) structure often exhibit poor accuracy at Dirichlet boundaries. To address these limitations, this paper introduces an additive kinematic decomposition at the continuous level, splitting the displacement and velocity fields into a relative dynamic component that vanishes on the boundary and a prescribed lifting function extending into the interior domain. This decomposition induces a distributed port that maps the effects of the boundary actuation inside the domain. By incorporating this mapping into suitable virtual power principles, we derive lifted port-Hamiltonian system (PHS) models that, upon finite element discretization, reduce to ODE systems in which Dirichlet boundary velocities are strongly imposed. The framework is applied to derive 2-field and 4-field formulations suited to distinct PHS geometric representations. Furthermore, we show that under specific shape functions, standard FEM schemes are recovered, demonstrating that the lifting framework in the discrete models is equivalent to the classic algebraic matrix partitioning in computational mechanics practice. The energy-balance properties and computational performance of the proposed methodology are verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [139] [Constrained minimization problems and FFT-based solvers: application to local Dirichlet boundary conditions and contact mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26082)
*Lionel Gélébart,Yaovi Armand Amouzou-Adou*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散三角函数变换（DTTs）的FFT求解器扩展框架，通过引入拉格朗日乘子法和约束优化，解决了传统FFT方法无法处理同一面混合边界条件、内部点Dirichlet边界条件及节点间运动学关系等问题，并将算法进一步推广至不等式约束以模拟接触力学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统基于FFT的求解器仅支持周期性边界条件的局限性，扩展其能力以处理任意类型的边界条件，特别是同一面上混合边界条件、域内点Dirichlet边界以及多点约束等复杂情况，从而拓宽FFT方法在计算力学中的应用范围。

Method: 以位移为基础结合DTTs，通过引入等式约束的约束最小化问题和拉格朗日乘子法，对离散局部方程进行修改；进而将算法推广至不等式约束以模拟接触力学问题。

Result: 通过紧凑拉伸类试件的模拟验证了算法的有效性；在接触力学方面，采用刚性球体无摩擦接触与Hertz理论进行对比，在微小压入深度/球体半径比值下结果吻合，有限应变与小应变比较显示随比值增大偏差增大。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法将显著扩展FFT求解器的应用领域，使其能够处理更广泛的边界条件和接触问题。

Abstract: The present paper focuses on a recent and active research axis to overcome the limitations of FFT-based solvers in order to apply various types of boundary conditions (BCs) and not only periodic BCs. A complete framework based on discrete trigonometric transforms (DTTs) of various types, possibly combined with discrete Fourier transform, has been proposed recently to account for any type of BCs defined per face of the unit-cell and per component of the displacement or traction vector. A parallel implementation by Amouzou-Adoun et al. recently proved its robustness and versatility. However, this approach is not able to account for a mix of BCs on a same face (for example Dirichlet BC on a part of a face and Neumann BC on the other part of the same face), neither to prescribe Dirichlet BC to points defined inside the domain, nor to define kinematic relations between displacement on different nodes. Starting from the displacement-based approach together with the use of DTTs, simple modifications are proposed to account for all these questions. The modified solver, first introduced from the viewpoint of discrete local equations, is then discussed from the perspective of constrained minimization with equality constraints and the introduction of Lagrange multipliers. Simulations of a compact tension-like specimen are performed and validated. To go further, the algorithm is then extended from equality constraints to account for inequality constraints to simulate contact mechanics. For the sake of validation, results obtained with a rigid spherical indenter with frictionless contact are compared to the Hertz theory for small ratios (indentation depth/sphere radius). Comparison between small and finite strains demonstrates increasing discrepancies when increasing this ratio. It is believed that the proposed methodology will significantly expand the field of applications of FFT-based solvers.

</details>


### [140] [When a positive SIMP density floor is not enough: solver admissibility and guarded floor selection in matrix-free 3D topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26382)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang,Yunsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了三维SIMP拓扑优化的矩阵无关几何多重网格FGMRES求解器中，报告收敛但实际未收敛的问题（4/102测试案例中真残差为容差的1.35-49.5倍，22/40未保护优化达到迭代上限）。提出了一种验证性控制方案：通过探测冻结状态、双特征选择底层策略、强制重计算真残差确保收敛，在98/102分类匹配基础上用残差守卫捕获全部4例漏报。相对于固定10^{-3}底层，该策略在24个可接受状态保留原始算子，严重随机状态下平均柔度变化31.0%/梯度0.340，优化设计下0.48%/0.008，耗时增加2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵无关几何多重网格FGMRES求解器在三维SIMP拓扑优化中会出现报告收敛但实际未收敛的问题，需要设计一个验证性控制方案来确保求解正确性并管理成本-保真度权衡。

Method: 提出验证性控制方案：(1) 在原始底层对冻结状态进行探测；(2) 利用两个残差特征选择首次尝试的底层；(3) 强制重计算真残差||f-Ku||/||f||≤10^{-6}作为验收标准。采用双特征规则（匹配98/102分类）配合残差守卫捕获4例漏报。

Result: 98/102参考分类匹配，残差守卫捕获全部4例漏报，102例求解均满足容差。相对固定10^{-3}底层策略：24个可接受状态保留原始算子，严重随机状态柔度变化31.0%/梯度0.340，优化设计0.48%/0.008，耗时增加2.5倍。8/12子集在传统10^{-6}底层仍需升级。禁用自适应预条件器后所有失败可见且无误接受。

Conclusion: 重计算残差是正确性保障；探测和底层梯级管理特定实现的成本-保真度权衡。残差估计漂移源于迭代依赖的预条件器（禁用自适应组件后所有失败可见）。

Abstract: In a matrix-free geometric-multigrid FGMRES solver for three-dimensional SIMP topology optimization, a reported converged solve is not always a converged solve. On four of 102 held-out states, the projected residual used for stopping falls below $10^{-6}$ while a recomputed true residual is 1.35 to 49.5 times the tolerance; in an unguarded optimization trajectory, 22 of 40 state solves reach the iteration cap without raising an error. We formulate floor selection as a verified control problem: probe the frozen state at the original floor, use two residual features to choose the first attempted floor, and accept no solution until $||f-Ku||/||f||\le10^{-6}$ is recomputed. The two-feature rule matches 98 of 102 reference classifications; the residual guard detects the four missed escalations, and all 102 selected solves satisfy the tolerance. Relative to always using a $10^{-3}$ floor, the policy preserves the original operator on 24 admissible states and avoids mean compliance and gradient changes of 31.0% and 0.340 on those severe random states, and 0.48% and 0.008 on seven optimized designs, at 2.5 times the mean wall time. In a 12-state subset of the held-out states, eight still require escalation at the conventional floor $10^{-6}$. In a nine-state control with the preconditioner's adaptive components disabled, every failure is visible and no false acceptance occurs, tying the stopping-estimate drift to the iterate-dependent preconditioner. The recomputed residual is the correctness safeguard; the probe and floor ladder govern an implementation-specific cost-fidelity tradeoff.

</details>


### [141] [First- and Second-Order Phase Transformation Modeling Based on the Hamilton Principle: A Coupled Thermo-Mechanical Approach for Glass Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26610)
*Tobias Rudolf,Meisam Soleimani,Philipp Junker*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文基于扩展哈密顿原理，建立了一个耦合热-力-相变的多物理场材料模型，用于描述玻璃增材制造中的极端热历史对微观结构和力学完整性的影响。数值验证表明该模型能复现不同冷却速率下的TTT行为，并通过有限元模拟揭示了激光沉积过程中残余应力与宏观翘曲的累积机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃增材制造中固有的极端加热和快速冷却热历史直接决定打印材料的微观结构和力学完整性，但缺乏能够统一描述这类复杂热-力-相变耦合过程的理论框架。

Method: 从扩展哈密顿原理出发，建立有限应变下热-力-相变耦合的统一变分框架；采用运动学分裂分别处理热膨胀、相变密度变化和粘弹性变形；引入一阶熔化与二阶玻璃转变的热力学描述；利用温度依赖粘度模型捕捉玻璃化转变中微观结构的动力学冻结；采用单片邻近单元法(NEM)求解热方程，并在ANSYS中开展三维有限元模拟。

Result: 材料点层面的数值验证表明模型能准确复现不同冷却速率下的时间-温度-转变(TTT)行为；三维有限元模拟揭示了激光沉积过程中相变动力学与粘弹性松弛相互作用导致的残余应力累积和宏观翘曲。

Conclusion: 所提出的多物理场材料模型能够有效预测玻璃增材制造中热历史诱导的微观结构演变与力学响应，为工艺优化和缺陷控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing of glass inherently involves complex thermal histories characterized by extreme heating and rapid cooling rates. These extreme conditions directly govern the final microstructure and mechanical integrity of the printed material. This work presents a comprehensive multi-physics material model derived from the extended Hamilton principle, establishing a unified variational framework for coupled thermal, mechanical, and phase transformation processes at finite strains. The formulation integrates a rigorous thermodynamic description of first-order melting and second-order glass transitions with a kinematic split accounting for thermal expansion, phase specific density changes, and viscoelastic deformation. A temperature dependent viscosity model is employed to capture the kinetic freezing of the microstructure inherent to vitrification. The numerical implementation utilizes a monolithic Neighbored Element Method (NEM) for the solution of the heat equation, ensuring computational efficiency and stability. Numerical investigations at the material point level validate the models ability to reproduce Time-Temperature-Transformation (TTT) behavior under varying cooling rates. Furthermore, three dimensional Finite Element simulations in ANSYS of a laser-based deposition process demonstrate the accumulation of residual stresses and macroscopic warpage resulting from the interplay between phase transformation kinetics and viscous relaxation.

</details>


### [142] [Not All Reads Are Conflicts: A Write-Only Analysis of the Sui Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26691)
*Haygen Tsoi,Alberto Sonnino,Philipp Jovanovic*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文针对Sui区块链的并行交易执行性能，提出“仅写集”（W-only）的依赖图模型来替代传统的“读写”（R+W）冲突图，从而更精确地衡量工作负载争用的上下界。分析2025年Sui主网数据发现：移除只读依赖后争用拓扑结构剧变、最优并行度高估约30-40%；DeepBook虽争用体积大但非顺序瓶颈；10-50%的网络美元价值流经顺序约束路径，存在排序风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sui的并行执行性能受工作负载争用限制，但此前基于R+W冲突图的分析因包含虚假边（只读依赖）而高估争用程度，需要更精确的争用度量方法。

Method: 提出W-only依赖图模型（仅含写序列化边）作为争用下界，与R+W模型（上界）联合界定真实执行依赖结构；使用并查集对象分组隔离关键合约（DeepBook）；对主网数据进行实证分析。

Result: 1）W-only下争用拓扑不再是“枢纽-辐条”结构，而是高度同配且以团（clique）为主，最优并行度高估减少30-40%；2）DeepBook虽争用体积大但不构成顺序瓶颈；3）10-50%的网络美元价值流经顺序约束路径。

Conclusion: R+W和W-only双模型可更准确刻画Sui的争用特征；DeepBook的争用可进一步优化；高价值交易暴露于排序风险，需引起关注。

Abstract: Sui's object-centric data model enables parallel transaction execution, but realised performance is fundamentally bounded by workload contention. Prior empirical analyses of Sui have relied on "read+write" (R+W) conflict graphs inherited from account-based blockchains. Because Sui's engine serialises only on mutable shared access, R+W graphs contain spurious edges, bounding contention from above. In this paper, we adopt a complementary "write-set-only" (W-only) model in which every edge represents a real write-serialisation event, providing a lower bound on contention. Together, the two models bracket Sui's true execution-dependency structure.
  Applying the W-only analysis to Sui mainnet data through 2025 yields three primary findings. First, removing read-only dependencies (notably the system clock) causes previously reported "hub-and-spoke" structures to collapse. The remaining contention topology is highly assortative and clique-dominated, with the W-only bound shaving roughly $30$--$40\%$ off the R+W estimate of Sui's optimal-parallelism headroom. Second, via union-find object grouping, we isolate DeepBook (Sui's native central limit order book). While it dominates contention by volume, its underlying logic does not impose disproportionate sequential bottlenecks. Finally, we quantify the economic cost of contention, showing that $10$--$50\%$ of the network's USD-denominated value flows through sequentially constrained execution paths, exposing it to potential ordering effects.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [The Code Distortion Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26261)
*Huck Bennett,Matthew Fox,Bryant Morrell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文推广了线性码等价概念，引入并研究了编码失真问题（CDP），证明其NP难近似性，并提出了指数时间近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性码之间的最小失真度，以量化两个码的相似性，并推广了在线性码等价问题（LCE）上的工作。

Method: 借鉴格失真问题（LDP）的分析技术，引入逐次极小基、矩阵0→0“范数”等概念，并改编了Goldreich等人的SVP到CVP的归约方法。

Result: 证明CDP的判定版本在任意常数因子近似下是NP难的，且属于Σ₂^P；给出了单指数时间k²近似算法及其特例下的改进算法。

Conclusion: CDP是LCE的自然推广，具有计算复杂性与近似算法方面的理论成果，相关概念可独立应用于编码理论。

Abstract: Two linear error-correcting codes $\cal{C}_1, \cal{C}_2 \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^n$ are called linearly equivalent if there is a linear isometry mapping $\cal{C}_1$ to $\cal{C}_2$. In this work, we generalize the notion of linear equivalence and study the minimum distortion $\cal{D}(\cal{C}_1, \cal{C}_2)$ of a linear mapping between codes $\cal{C}_1, \cal{C}_2 \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^n$, which quantifies how similar $\cal{C}_1$ and $\cal{C}_2$ are. We introduce and study the Code Distortion Problem (CDP), which asks to find a minimum distortion mapping between two input codes $\cal{C}_1$ and $\cal{C}_2$. CDP generalizes the Linear Code Equivalence Problem (LCE), which is essentially the special case of CDP where $\cal{D}(\cal{C}_1, C_2) = 1$ and which is well-studied because of its role in cryptography.
  We prove that (decisional) CDP is $\mathsf{NP}$-hard to approximate to within any constant factor, and that it is in $Σ_2^P$. We also give a single-exponential-time $k^2$-approximation algorithm for CDP, where $k$ is the dimension of the input codes. Furthermore, we give a single-exponential-time $\big(\frac{2k + 1}{3})^2$-approximation algorithm for a natural special case of CDP, and we show that our analysis is tight in this case.
  We use techniques from analogous work on the Lattice Distortion Problem (LDP) by Bennett, Dadush, and Stephens-Davidowitz (ESA, 2016). We also introduce or study a number of additional concepts that might be of independent interest. These include an adaptation of the celebrated reduction of Goldreich, Micciancio, Safra, and Seifert (IPL, 1999) from the Shortest Vector Problem (SVP) to the Closest Vector Problem (CVP) on lattices to the analogous problems on codes; successive minima bases for codes; and the matrix $0 \to 0$ "norm" on subspaces.

</details>


### [144] [Polynomially Improved Lower Bounds for Trifferent Codes via Locally Sparse $3$-Uniform Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26376)
*Xuejiao Han,Yubo Sun,Gennian Ge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过改进Körner-Marton级联构造中的外码步骤，将三不同码的下界从常数因子改进为$c\sqrt{n}(9/5)^{n/4}$，主要技术包括对3-均匀超图进行随机稀疏化并移除Berge二环和三环，利用Verstraete-Wilson定理得到局部稀疏超图的大独立集，从而获得$\sqrt{n}$因子提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Körner-Marton构造给出了三不同码大小的指数级下界$T(n)\ge c_0(9/5)^{n/4}$，但该下界中的常数因子$c_0$可以进一步改进，本文旨在证明一个多项式级别的强化下界。

Method: (1) 将非分离三元组编码为3-均匀超图的边；(2) 随机稀疏化顶点集；(3) 移除高度数顶点及所有剩余的Berge二环和三环；(4) 得到局部稀疏超图后，应用Verstraete-Wilson定理获得大独立集，从而产生$\sqrt{n}$因子；(5) 与长度4的Tetra码级联得到最终下界。

Result: 证明了下界$T(n)\ge c\sqrt{n}(9/5)^{n/4}$，其中$c>0$为绝对常数，相较于经典结果增加了$\sqrt{n}$的多项式因子。

Conclusion: 通过精细化的超图稀疏化技术成功改进了三不同码的下界，展示了随机方法和超图理论在编码理论中的有效应用。

Abstract: A ternary code is \emph{trifferent} if every three distinct codewords have a coordinate in which their symbols are pairwise distinct. Let $T(n)$ be the maximum size of a trifferent code of length $n$. The classical Körner--Marton construction gives $T(n)\ge c_0(9/5)^{n/4}$ for an absolute constant $c_0>0$. We prove the polynomial strengthening $T(n)\ge c\sqrt{n}(9/5)^{n/4}$ for an absolute constant $c>0$. Our proof refines the outer-code step in the Körner--Marton concatenation. We encode non separating triples as edges of a $3$-uniform hypergraph, randomly thin its vertex set, and remove high-degree vertices together with all remaining Berge cycles of lengths two and three. The resulting locally sparse hypergraph admits a large independent set by a theorem of Verstraete and Wilson, producing the additional factor $\sqrt n$. Concatenation with the length-four Tetra code then yields the stated lower bound.

</details>


### [145] [Upper bounds on the length of quasi-MDS codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26684)
*Umberto Martínez-Peñas,Rubén Rodríguez-Ballesteros*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了折叠汉明距离下$\mathbb F_q$-线性QMDS码的长度上界，通过将码与子空间族对应，将长度问题转化为1-子空间堆积的上界问题，并利用部分展开的归约方法从有限几何中引入尖界，恢复了Ball等人的Griesmer型上界，并在多个参数区域获得了更紧的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究折叠汉明距离下$\mathbb F_q$-线性QMDS码的长度上界与域大小$q$等参数的关系，以获得更紧的码长上界。

Method: 建立QMDS码与子空间族之间的对应关系，将长度上界问题转化为1-子空间堆积的上界问题；通过归约到部分展开（partial spreads），引入Drake-Freeman、Năstase-Sissokho、Honold-Kiermaier-Kurz等有限几何中的尖界。

Result: 恢复了Ball等人关于QMDS码的Griesmer型上界，并在多个参数区间获得了更紧的上界。

Conclusion: 通过子空间堆积与有限几何工具，给出了折叠汉明距离下QMDS码长度的改进上界。

Abstract: We study upper bounds on the length of $\mathbb F_q$-linear QMDS codes in the folded Hamming distance relative to their other parameters, especially the field size $q$. Via a correspondence between such codes and families of subspaces, we relate the length problem to that of upper bounding $1$-subspace packings with respect to the other parameters, especially the field size. Our main result is a reduction from these families to partial spreads, which allows us to import sharp bounds from finite geometry, including results of Drake-Freeman, Năstase-Sissokho, and Honold-Kiermaier-Kurz. As a consequence, we recover the Griesmer-type upper bound on the length of QMDS codes by Ball et al. and obtain tighter upper bounds in several parameter regimes.

</details>


### [146] [Self-dual double cyclic codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26823)
*Ricky Aditya,Aleams Barra,Djoko Suprijanto*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_q$ 上自对偶双循环码的结构与存在性，给出了生成元生成自对偶码的充要条件，并针对特定长度提供了构造方法和显式例子，同时揭示了与其他自对偶码类的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解自对偶双循环码的结构特征，填补该领域在充要条件与存在性方面的理论空白。

Method: 将双循环码建模为 $\mathbb{F}_q[x]$-子模，通过两个多项式对刻画生成元；利用生成元性质推导自对偶的充要条件；针对 $(r,r)$、$(r,2r)$、$(2r,r)$ 及 $\gcd(r,s)=1$ 的情形分别设计构造方法，并给出有限域上的显式例子。

Result: 给出了两个多项式对生成自对偶双循环码的充要条件；证明了特定长度下自对偶双循环码的存在性，并提供了系统性的构造方法；展示了所构造码与经典自对偶码类之间的关联。

Conclusion: 自对偶双循环码具有丰富的代数结构，通过多项式对刻画可有效实现存在性判定与显式构造，且与已知自对偶码类存在深层联系。

Abstract: This article focuses specifically on the study of self-dual double cyclic codes over a finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$. A self-dual double cyclic code is a double cyclic code that is equal to its dual. Structurally, a double cyclic code of length $(r,s)$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$ is a $\mathbb{F}_q[x]$-submodule of $\mathbb{F}_{q,r,s}:=\mathbb{F}_q[x]/\langle x^r-1\rangle\times\mathbb{F}_q[x]/\langle x^s-1\rangle$. Moreover, any double cyclic code of length $(r,s)$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$ is generated by two pairs of polynomials in $\mathbb{F}_{q,r,s}$. From the properties of the generating elements, we provide the necessary and sufficient conditions such that two pairs of polynomials in $\mathbb{F}_{q,r,s}$ generate a self-dual code. Furthermore, we examine the existence of self-dual double cyclic codes for some specific lengths: $(r,r)$; $(r,2r)$ and $(2r,r)$; and $(r,s)$, where $\gcd(r,s)=1$. For each case, we provide a construction method with some explicit examples over various finite fields. We also observe some connections between self-dual double cyclic codes and other classes of self-dual codes.

</details>


### [147] [Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes: Characterizations, Bounds, and Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27091)
*Yang Li,San Ling,Zhenliang Lu,Gaojun Luo,Shixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码（EAQLRCs），通过利用预共享无噪声纠缠对，放松了传统CSS和Hermitian构造中对偶包含或自正交的约束，从而允许使用更多经典LRCs来构造量子LRCs。论文建立了充分的支持条件、CSS-like构造、局部性上界和Singleton-like界，并给出了最优纯EAQLRCs的通用构造框架，由此得到两个最优纯CSS-like EAQLRCs族，据我们所知这是首个显式的EAQLRCs族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子局部可恢复码（qLRCs）受限于CSS和Hermitian构造中的对偶包含或自正交约束，导致许多结构良好的经典LRCs无法用于构造qLRCs。为放松这些约束，作者引入纠缠辅助的量子局部可恢复码（EAQLRCs）。

Method: 假设预共享最大纠缠对的一半无噪声，刻画扩展稳定子具有局部性r的充分支持条件；从两个经典码导出CSS-like构造，无需对偶包含条件；建立局部性上界和Singleton-like界；利用ℓ-交集MDS码对和分块奇偶校验矩阵构造两个最优纯CSS-like EAQLRCs族。

Result: 建立了EAQLRCs的充分支持条件、CSS-like构造、局部性上界和Singleton-like界，刻画了达到后者等号的纯码。通过ℓ-交集MDS码对和分块奇偶校验矩阵，得到两个具有灵活参数和非平凡局部性的最优纯CSS-like EAQLRCs族，据我们所知这是首个显式的EAQLRCs族。

Conclusion: EAQLRCs为构造量子局部可恢复码提供了更灵活的框架，放松了传统约束，能够从经典LRCs构造最优量子LRCs，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) allow a single qudit erasure to be corrected by accessing only a small number of other qudits. Standard CSS and Hermitian constructions, however, impose dual-containing or self-orthogonal constraints on the underlying classical codes, thereby restricting the well-structured classical LRCs (cLRCs) that can be used to construct qLRCs. To relax these constraints, we introduce entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes (EAQLRCs) by assuming that halves of the pre-shared maximally entangled pairs are noiseless. We characterize sufficient support conditions on extended stabilizers under which entanglement-assisted stabilizer codes have locality $r$ and derive a CSS-like construction from two classical codes without imposing the ordinary dual-containing condition. We further establish an upper bound on locality and a Singleton-like bound for arbitrary CSS-like EAQLRCs, and characterize the pure codes attaining equality in the latter bound. These results yield a general framework for constructing optimal pure EAQLRCs from pairs of cLRCs. Applying this framework to $\ell$-intersection pairs of MDS codes and block parity-check matrices, we obtain two families of optimal pure CSS-like EAQLRCs with flexible parameters and nontrivial localities. To the best of our knowledge, these represent the first explicit families of EAQLRCs.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Two2Four: Generative Quadruped Puppeteering from Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26108)
*Fatemeh Zargarbashi,Zehong Qiu,Dhruv Agrawal,Stelian Coros,Robert W. Sumner,Martin Guay,Jakob Buhmann*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种从普通人体运动数据自动生成可控四足动物运动的框架，采用两阶段生成扩散模型，支持行走、奔跑、跳跃、坐、躺等多种动作，并可精细控制头部和四肢运动，在运动真实感和可控性上优于现有重定向方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的四足动物运动生成方法（动作捕捉训练有素的表演者或从人体运动重定向）成本高、难度大，且难以复现自然动物运动的细微之处，因此需要数据驱动的替代方案。

Method: 采用两阶段生成扩散模型，仅用四足动物运动数据训练；通过结构化条件注入和修复策略，支持多种动作模式；提供头部运动控制和单个肢体操控等细粒度控制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在运动真实感和可控性方面优于现有重定向方法，可有效用于动画和虚拟制作。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种有效的自动人体到四足动物运动映射工具，能够生成真实且可控的四足动物运动，适用于动画和虚拟制作应用。

Abstract: Realistic animal motion for virtual production is typically obtained either through motion capture of highly trained performers who accurately mimic animal behavior, or by retargeting ordinary human motion using complex control setups. Both approaches are challenging and often fail to fully reproduce the nuances of natural animal motion, motivating data-driven alternatives. We present an automatic human-to-quadruped puppeteering framework that produces plausible and controllable quadruped motions from ordinary human motion data. Our approach employs a two-stage generative diffusion model trained purely on quadruped motion data. By introducing a structured conditioning and inpainting strategy, our method supports a wide range of actions, including walking, running, jumping, sitting, and lying. Furthermore, we enable fine-grained intuitive control of the quadruped motion such as head movement control and individual limb puppeteering. Experimental results demonstrate improved motion realism and controllability compared to existing retargeting approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of our framework as a tool for animation and virtual production applications.

</details>


### [149] [Global Pass Barriers Without Per-Resource RHI Tracking: A Cross-Vendor Study with Blade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26506)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文通过Blade与wgpu的对比实验，系统研究了显式图形API中内存依赖、屏障放置与状态追踪的性能开销。在六款GPU（含Apple/Metal）上的测量表明，移除冗余屏障可显著降低计算与渲染耗时，但存在稳定性下限；不追踪资源的全局屏障范围推导在部分GPU上节省了5-6.7%的开销。RADV源码分析揭示了命令数与实际开销的非对应关系，并验证了持久化GENERAL布局在RDNA条件下保留DCC但禁用FMASK。最终提出的Blade方向是：在无追踪的RHI中采用轻量级聚合pass-kind状态，由上游渲染图选择全局依赖切割，而资源感知的引擎层负责别名、跨队列、异常布局和任意per-resource DAG边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式图形API（如Vulkan）要求开发者手动管理内存依赖、资源访问状态和图像布局，这导致状态追踪和屏障放置的开销难以预估。现有实现如wgpu采用逐资源追踪验证，而Blade采用全局屏障策略，两者在性能上的量化对比和优化空间尚不明确。本研究旨在隔离屏障放置与阶段/访问作用域的影响，通过端到端对比找出最优策略。

Method: 设计对比实验，在Blade中隔离屏障放置和阶段/访问作用域逻辑，与wgpu的逐资源追踪方案进行端到端比较。在六款来自四家供应商的GPU上测量，包含Apple/Metal的探索性结果。通过移除冗余屏障、推导全局屏障作用域等方式测试性能变化，并结合RADV源码分析底层驱动行为。

Result: 移除15个冗余屏障：RTX 5070计算耗时降29.3%，RX 7900 XT降32.3%；独立渲染耗时分别降32.4%和7.3%；但Radeon 780M在32 pass时增42.4%，存在12.3%的稳定性下限。全局屏障范围推导在NVIDIA图形链节省5.0%，AMD计算链节省6.7%；AMD渲染相关作用域均未达标。wgpu的记录与提交成本在所有测试单元中均更高。RADV显示全局依赖会扩展为多个flush/invalidate请求，且持久化GENERAL保留DCC但禁用FMASK。

Conclusion: 无资源追踪的轻量级全局pass-kind状态方案可行，但需由上游渲染图选择全局依赖切割点。别名、跨队列使用、异常布局和任意per-resource DAG边等复杂场景仍需资源感知的引擎层处理。Blade的最终方向应是在无追踪RHI中采用聚合pass-kind状态，结合渲染图与引擎层的分工协作。

Abstract: Explicit graphics APIs expose memory dependencies, per-resource accesses, and image layouts. wgpu reconstructs and validates this state; Blade keeps Vulkan images in GENERAL, tracks no per-resource state, and issues global pass-boundary barriers. We isolate barrier placement and stage/access scope within Blade, compare matched wgpu programs end-to-end, and measure six GPUs from four vendors, including exploratory Apple/Metal results.
  Removing fifteen redundant barriers from sixteen independent compute passes reduces GPU span by 29.3% on an RTX 5070 and 32.3% on an RX 7900 XT. It reduces independent-render span by 32.4% and 7.3% on the same discrete parts, but increases Radeon 780M span by 42.4% at 32 passes, beyond a 12.3% count-specific stability floor. No dependent-chain placement effect clears the study's cell-specific stability criterion. Deriving global barrier scope from the pass kinds around each boundary, without tracking any resource, saves 5.0% on an NVIDIA graphics chain and 6.7% on an AMD compute chain; no AMD render-involving scope cell resolves. wgpu's record-and-submit cost is higher in every measured cell, but this end-to-end difference cannot be attributed to tracking alone.
  RADV source shows why command counts do not predict these costs: broad global dependencies expand to several flush and invalidate requests that the driver may partly elide. It also shows that persistent GENERAL retains DCC under the measured RDNA conditions but disables FMASK. The resulting Blade direction is lightweight aggregate pass-kind state in a tracking-free RHI: an upstream render graph selects global dependency cuts, while aliasing, cross-queue use, exceptional layouts, and arbitrary per-resource DAG edges remain resource-aware engine responsibilities.

</details>


### [150] [AtlasLC: Fast Codec-Ready Compression of Object-Centric 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26525)
*ByungHyun Kim,Jinwoo Jeon,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: AtlasLC是一种面向对象级3D高斯泼溅的无源、免训练压缩管道，通过局部竞争剪枝与确定性图集打包相结合，在无需原始训练数据的情况下，显著降低图集准备时间和端到端压缩时间，同时保持压缩比、解码延迟、运行帧率和几何质量之间的部署感知平衡，适用于可扩展的XR资产库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法主要针对场景级数据设计，依赖繁重的布局生成或全局剪枝，难以适应语义集中的前景对象；且在XR实际资产管线中，对象级3DGS的压缩需要同时考虑资产准备成本、编解码兼容性、解码延迟以及深度和轮廓线索的保持，而现有方法未能在这些部署感知维度上提供平衡优化。

Method: AtlasLC包含两个核心组件：1) 局部竞争剪枝（local-competition pruning），移除映射/重映射瓶颈同时保留对象宽的前景支撑；2) 确定性图集打包（deterministic atlas packing）。两者通过轻量级单遍排序的条件传输（single-pass sort-based conditional transport）作为共享坐标骨干进行耦合。整个管道无需原始图像、相机位姿或逐资产优化，直接对发布的Gaussian资产进行操作。

Result: 在评估的资产上，AtlasLC将图集准备时间最多降低25倍，端到端压缩时间最多降低5倍；相较于同等紧凑的结构化基线，比特率降低约6-8%，同时保持可比的感知质量和几何质量。

Conclusion: 对象级3DGS压缩应针对部署感知的工作点进行优化，以实现可扩展的XR资产库。AtlasLC表明，在无源、免训练的条件下，通过局部竞争剪枝与确定性图集打包的组合，可以在压缩效率、解码速度、渲染质量和几何保持之间取得有利的权衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic novel-view synthesis with real-time rendering, but deploying compressed object-centric 3DGS in XR requires more than image-space rate-distortion. In practical XR asset pipelines, reusable objects are repeatedly packaged, transmitted, decoded, and instantiated, making asset-preparation cost, codec compatibility, decoding latency, and preservation of depth and silhouette cues first-class concerns. Existing 3DGS compression methods are largely developed for scene-scale captures and often rely on heavy layout generation or aggressive global pruning, assumptions that transfer poorly to semantically concentrated foreground objects. We present AtlasLC, a source-free, training-free compression pipeline for object-centric 3DGS that operates directly on released Gaussian assets, without original images, camera poses, or per-asset optimization. AtlasLC couples local-competition pruning with deterministic atlas packing to remove the mapping/remapping bottleneck while preserving object-wide foreground support; a lightweight single-pass sort-based conditional transport is used as a shared coordinate backbone for these stages. Across the evaluated assets, AtlasLC reduces atlas-preparation time by up to a factor of 25 and end-to-end compression time by up to a factor of 5, while offering a favorable deployment-aware balance of payload, decode latency, runtime FPS, and 3D geometry relative to the evaluated compressed baselines. Relative to similarly compact structured baselines, it uses about 6 to 8 percent fewer bits while maintaining comparable perceptual and geometric quality. These results show that object-centric 3DGS compression should be optimized for a deployment-aware operating point enabling scalable XR asset libraries.

</details>


### [151] [StructureGS: Structure-aware Gaussian Splatting for Articulated Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26889)
*Gahye Lee,Gyoonseo Kim,Wonjong Jang,Jooeun Son,Seungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出StructureGS框架，通过将结构感知引导融入3D高斯溅射，利用部件有向包围盒施加空间一致性和结构连通性约束，实现可动部件关节物体的高质量重建，在部件分解和几何质量上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖光度监督，难以解耦关节物体的几何、外观和运动参数，导致部件分解边界模糊且存在几何伪影。

Method: 将结构感知引导引入3D高斯溅射，利用部件有向包围盒施加两类约束：空间一致性（约束部件几何紧致且空间连贯）和结构连通性（约束相邻部件间物理合理的接触关系），通过结构感知损失注入显式结构约束。

Result: 在关节物体重建任务上达到SOTA性能，生成了具有清晰部件几何的高质量结果。

Conclusion: 显式注入结构约束能够有效解耦几何、外观与运动参数，提升关节物体重建中部件分解的质量与物理合理性。

Abstract: Reconstructing articulated objects with multiple movable parts is essential for understanding object structure and enabling physical interaction. However, this reconstruction task poses significant challenges due to the entanglement of geometry, appearance, and motion parameters during optimization. Existing methods rely primarily on photometric supervision, which commonly fails to disentangle these interdependent components, resulting in poor part decomposition with blurred boundaries and geometric artifacts. To address this limitation, we introduce StructureGS, a reconstruction framework for articulated objects that integrates structure-aware guidance into 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our approach leverages oriented bounding boxes of object parts to enforce two key structural properties: spatial coherence, which constrains each part's geometry to remain compact and spatially coherent within its designated region, and structural connectivity, which enforces physically plausible contact relationships between adjacent parts. These properties are realized through structure-aware losses that inject explicit structural constraints into the optimization process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in articulated object reconstruction, producing high-quality results with well-defined part geometries.

</details>


### [152] [Convex Collision-Free Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26901)
*Tomoyo Kikuchi,Takashi Kanai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: CCFR是一种显式表示局部凸可行区域来处理碰撞的方法，为每个顶点独立构建可行区域，不仅能处理主碰撞，还能处理次级碰撞和共维接触，具有高度可扩展性和并行性，不依赖非线性优化，兼容XPBD等仿真框架，在布料、毛发、线、粒子系统和共维接触场景中展现了高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碰撞处理方法大多依赖隐式可行性表示，导致在处理次级碰撞和共维接触时鲁棒性不足，或与特定非线性优化方案紧密耦合，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。

Method: CCFR从周围网格基元配置（边-边和顶点-面交互）中显式构造局部凸可行区域，为每个顶点独立构建穿透前定义的可行区域，编码几何非穿透约束，与物理接触响应模型解耦。

Result: 在布料、毛发、线、粒子系统和共维接触等多种场景中验证了CCFR的有效性，展示了其通用且高效的碰撞处理能力。

Conclusion: CCFR提供了一种独立于非线性优化且与多种仿真框架兼容的碰撞处理方法，通过显式凸可行区域表示，在保证鲁棒性的同时实现了高度可扩展的并行碰撞处理。

Abstract: Convex Collision-Free Regions (CCFR) is a collision handling method that explicitly represents local convex feasible regions to enforce non-penetration. Each feasible region is constructed from surrounding mesh primitive configurations, including edge-edge and vertex-face interactions. The resulting convex region represents admissible non-penetrating vertex displacements at the current configuration. Existing collision handling methods for deformable body simulation have largely relied on implicit representations of feasibility, resulting in either compromised robustness for secondary collisions and codimensional contacts or tight coupling with specific nonlinear optimization schemes. Our formulation constructs feasible regions independently for each vertex, defined prior to penetration, inherently accounts not only for primary collisions but also for secondary collisions and codimensional contacts, enabling highly scalable and parallelizable collision handling. These feasible regions encode geometric non-penetration constraints independently of physical contact response models. CCFR does not rely on nonlinear optimization and is compatible with simulation frameworks such as Extended Position-Based Dynamics (XPBD) that do not explicitly maintain interior feasibility during iterative updates. The effectiveness of CCFR is demonstrated across cloth, hair, wire, particle systems, and codimensional contact scenarios, showing versatile and efficient collision handling.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Collusion with Competitive Marginals: Price-Level Audits Are Blind by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26385)
*Xin Xu,Chengrui Wu,Jiayu Lu,Kaizhen Tan,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明，即使定价非合谋（竞争性定价），竞标代理之间通过未解释的投标分量的联合分布耦合也能实现有利可图的合谋，且现有基于单一代理价格历史的检测方法对此完全无效。实证发现：真实语言模型代理存在残差相关（+0.053 vs +0.0001），该耦合随采样温度上升单调下降（p=0.002），且在以太坊区块构建拍卖中，诚实投标人之间的依赖足够强，使得检测阈值高达+0.50至+0.81，无法区分合法多身份运营与合谋，监管目标应从检测转向计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法合谋实证工作仅关注价格是否超竞争性（supracompetitive），但作者指出，即使价格处于竞争水平，合谋仍可能有利可图。因此，需要揭示当前检测方法在面对特定耦合策略时的根本盲区。

Method: 理论分析：证明若投标代理仅通过未解释投标分量的联合分布耦合，而每个代理的投标边际分布保持竞争性，则任何基于单一代理价格/投标历史的检验功效等于假阳性率。实证验证：测试20个语言模型（19个独立开发者）的残差相关性；分析以太坊区块构建拍卖中77,684个投标来自39个投标者的依赖结构。

Result: 语言模型实验：同一模型两次部署间残差相关+0.053，不同模型间+0.0001，95%置信区间[0.030, 0.078]；耦合随采样温度上升单调下降（p=0.002）。以太坊拍卖：诚实投标者间的依赖导致5%假阳性率下的检测阈值高达+0.50至+0.81（家庭式采样阈值的20-32倍），且不随审计窗口增长而下降。将40个投标身份解析为23个运营商使Herfindahl指数提升247.5%，加入行为聚类后达324.5%。

Conclusion: 当前检测方法对通过联合分布耦合实现的有害合谋完全失效，这是结构性的而非样本量可修复。由于合法多身份运营与合谋在行为上不可区分，务实的监管目标应从检测合谋转向计数实际运营者数量。

Abstract: Empirical work on algorithmic collusion asks one question of the data: are prices supracompetitive? We show this can be answered "no" by a conspiracy that is nonetheless profitable. Consider bidding agents that couple only through the joint distribution of their unexplained bid components, leaving every agent's own bid law exactly at the competitive law. Any test whose input is a single agent's price or bid history then has power exactly equal to its false-positive rate, for every coupling strength up to comonotonicity. The published detection methodology is therefore blind to this conduct by construction rather than underpowered, and no sample size repairs it.
  Three empirical results follow. First, the mechanism appears in real language-model agents: twenty models from nineteen independent developers, three deployment prompts each, show residual correlation of $+0.053$ between two deployments of one model against $+0.0001$ across models, with a 95% interval clustered by developer of $[0.030, 0.078]$, under an auditor that sees every order feature and is fitted out of sample. Second, the coupling falls monotonically as sampling temperature rises ($p=0.002$), turning a deployment parameter into a candidate mitigation. Third, on 24 days of Ethereum block-building auction data covering 77,684 bids from 39 bidders, the honest population of bidder pairs is itself so dependent that a screen held at a 5% false-positive rate must sit above a floor of $+0.50$ to $+0.81$, which is 20 to 32 times the family-wise sampling threshold and does not fall as the audit window grows. Since lawful multi-identity operation and conspiracy are behaviourally indistinguishable here, the tractable regulatory target is not detection but counting: resolving 40 bidding identities into 23 operators raises the Herfindahl index by 247.5%, and adding behavioural clusters from public bid streams reaches 324.5%.

</details>


### [154] [Sensitivity and Differential Privacy in Metric Voting with Distortion below Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26388)
*Shinsaku Sakaue,Kaito Fujii,Soh Kumabe,Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在度量失真框架下，随机投票规则如何在打破失真界限3的同时，保持低敏感性和差分隐私。通过Gibbs分布族构造，实现了失真低于3、敏感度O((log m+1)/n)的随机规则，以及满足(O((log m+log(1/δ)+1)/n), δ)-差分隐私的变体规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机投票规则能否在打破确定性规则最优失真界限3的基础上，同时具备低敏感性和差分隐私保证，即在效用、稳定性和隐私之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于有偏度量视角，使用Gibbs分布族在常数规模的候选名单上构建随机规则，通过调节温度参数分别实现敏感性和差分隐私保证，并证明了有偏度量比的稳定性性质。

Result: 构造了失真不超过3-ε（ε为绝对常数）的随机规则，其最坏情况敏感度为O((log m+1)/n)；同时构造了满足(O((log m+log(1/δ)+1)/n), δ)-差分隐私的变体规则，同样达到失真3-ε。

Conclusion: 随机投票规则可以在打破失真3界限的同时，实现低敏感性和差分隐私，为度量失真框架下的隐私-效用权衡提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Voting rules aggregate individual preferences into collective decisions, but the rankings they receive contain only ordinal information. The metric distortion framework studies ordinal voting rules in settings where voters and candidates are embedded in an unknown metric space. Deterministic rules have optimal worst-case distortion $3$, while recent randomized rules break the $3$ barrier. We study whether such improvements can coexist with low worst-case sensitivity with respect to the Wasserstein distance of lotteries under one-voter deletion and approximate differential privacy under one-voter replacement. On the sensitivity side, we give a randomized rule with distortion at most $3-\varepsilon$ for an absolute constant $\varepsilon>0$ and, for $m$ candidates and $n$ voters, a worst-case sensitivity bound of $O((\log m+1)/n)$. On the privacy side, for every $δ\in(0,1)$ and all $n$ above an absolute constant, we construct a variant rule whose mechanism releasing a single sampled winner has distortion at most $3-\varepsilon$ and is $(O((\log m+\log(1/δ)+1)/n),δ)$-differentially private. Both constructions use the same family of Gibbs distributions over constant-size candidate lists, with only the temperature parameter differing between the sensitivity and differential-privacy guarantees. Our analysis builds on the biased-metric viewpoint behind the recent improvement over the $3$ barrier and proves a stability property for the biased-metric ratio.

</details>


### [155] [From Individual to Shared Ownership: A Coalitional Game Approach to Sustainable Co-investment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26725)
*Yue Yu,Andrea Araldo,Tijani Chahed,Rosario Patanè*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种合作博弈论框架，用于在监管激励下实现共享基础设施的可持续共同投资。该框架将共同投资问题建模为可转移效用（TU）联盟博弈，并证明可转化为线性生产博弈（LPG），通过对偶价格实现合作盈余的稳定分配。通过数据中心运营商在共享可再生能源基础设施中的共同投资案例验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多个异构运营商在监管激励下对共享基础设施进行可持续共同投资时，如何协调个体经济利益与联盟整体目标的问题。

Method: 将共同投资问题建模为可转移效用（TU）联盟博弈，考虑异构运营特征、动态资源可用性、投资成本和监管激励水平；证明该博弈可转化为线性生产博弈（LPG），利用对偶价格构建稳定且具有建设性的合作盈余分配方案。

Result: 所提出的联盟博弈可被重新表述为线性生产博弈，其对偶价格能够产生稳定且具有建设性的合作盈余分配。

Conclusion: 通过数据中心运营商在政府补贴支持下的共享可再生能源基础设施共同投资案例，验证了该框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a cooperative game-theoretic framework for sustainable co-investment in shared infrastructure under regulatory incentives. Multiple heterogeneous operators co-invest in a common infrastructure whose production capability evolves over time and is subject to operational variability. A regulator supports the deployment through incentive mechanisms designed to align individual economic investment objectives with the coalitional one. We formulate the co-investment problem as a transferable-utility (TU) coalitional game in which the value generated by cooperation depends on heterogeneous operational profiles, dynamic resource availability, investment costs, and regulatory incentive level. We show that the proposed coalitional game can be reformulated as a linear production game (LPG), whose dual prices yield a constructive and stable allocation of the cooperative surplus. Finally, we illustrate the proposed framework through a case study on co-investment among data center operators in shared renewable energy infrastructure, supported by government subsidies promoting renewable energy consumption.

</details>


### [156] [Stable and Budget-Feasible Coalition Formation for Clustered Federated Learning: A Hedonic Potential-Game Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26788)
*Cengis Hasan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可转移剩余模型的聚类联邦学习机制，通过货币转移和偏好分配实现稳定聚类，并证明了纳什稳定划分的存在性、势博弈性质以及福利最优性保证，在CIFAR-10实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类联邦学习将异构参与者组织成联盟并训练联盟专属模型，但这样的聚类只有在参与者偏好其被分配的联盟且所需转移支付可负担时才是可持续的。本文旨在解决聚类联邦学习中的稳定性与激励兼容问题。

Method: 建立可转移剩余模型，分离学习收益、系统成本、参与者成本和货币转移；通过分配规则将联盟剩余转化为享乐偏好；引入弱预算可行性保证协调者剩余非负。证明对称成对分配下诱导的博弈为精确势博弈，存在纳什稳定划分，严格更好响应过程收敛。将福利分解为参与者势和保留松弛，推导加性和乘性稳定价格保证。证明全局势最大化等价于加权最大一致性关联聚类，并通过显式构造实现端到端福利界。

Result: 证明了纳什稳定划分存在性及更好响应收敛性；刻画了有界成对激励的可行性，可在多项式时间内验证指数多个预算约束；推导了加性和乘性价格稳定保证（后者渐近紧）；精确平衡仅在成对可表示类上给出福利最优稳定性；预算可行性单独允许无界福利损失。CIFAR-10五种子实验表明机制在所有主实例上达到认证估计表福利最优，而均等剩余共享在三个实例上无纳什稳定结果。

Conclusion: 本文为聚类联邦学习提供了理论完备的激励兼容聚类机制，结合合作博弈论与享乐博弈，给出了稳定性、可计算性和福利最优性的系统性保证，实验验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: Clustered federated learning benefits from organizing heterogeneous participants into coalitions that train coalition-specific models, but such clustering is sustainable only if participants prefer their assigned coalition and the required transfers are affordable. We develop a transferable-surplus model separating learning benefit, system cost, participant cost, and monetary transfers; an allocation rule converts coalition surplus into hedonic preferences, and weak budget feasibility guarantees nonnegative retained coordinator surplus. For symmetric pairwise allocations the induced game is an exact potential game: a Nash-stable partition exists, every strict better-response process converges, and with destination consent accepted better responses reach an individually stable partition. We characterize feasibility of bounded pair incentives and verify the exponentially many budget constraints in polynomial oracle time when retained slack is submodular. Decomposing welfare into participant potential and retained slack yields additive and multiplicative price-of-stability guarantees, the latter asymptotically tight; exact balance gives welfare-optimal stability only on the pairwise-representable class, and budget feasibility alone permits unbounded welfare loss. Global potential maximization equals weighted maximum-agreement correlation clustering, and approximation followed by stabilization satisfies an end-to-end welfare bound governed by retained slack and negative-edge mass, attained by an explicit construction. In a preregistered five-seed CIFAR-10 study the mechanism reaches the certified estimated-table welfare optimum on every primary instance, equal-surplus sharing has no Nash-stable outcome on three, and pairwise validation gain gives far more reliable pair signs than gradient alignment.

</details>


### [157] [Correlated Chance Sampling for Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27035)
*Boning Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出CCS-MCCFR，用持久化Weyl流替代独立同分布机会采样，使每个具体机会节点在前N次访问中实现确定性局部频率误差O(log(N+1)/N)，相比i.i.d.的O(N^{-1/2})期望尺度有本质提升，在多种扑克游戏中将最终可剥削性降低19.05%至34.01%，且无额外超参数和可测量的时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCCFR在策略演化过程中反复分配机会结果，但每次访问独立采样机会节点，导致局部频率误差收敛缓慢（仅O(N^{-1/2})期望尺度），限制了策略学习的效率。

Method: 为每个具体机会节点分配一个持久的随机化Weyl流，通过其相位映射到节点的机会分布；固定索引的每次抽取保持正确边际分布，同时前N次访问的局部频率误差达到确定性O(log(N+1)/N)。在固定策略轨迹上保持无偏性，并通过条件标量界隔离自适应相位选择，每次遍历重置变体保留标准O(1/√T)外采样保证。

Result: 在Kuhn扑克和四种Leduc扑克配置中，最终可剥削性降低19.05%至34.01%，所有配对自助法置信区间均高于零；Goofspiel-4上显著降低4.27%；增益持续到300万次Leduc节点接触，且与Linear CFR结合达到最低可剥削性。

Conclusion: CCS-MCCFR通过仅修改机会采样器一行代码，在不引入新超参数和无时间开销的条件下，将显式局部保证和大规模可剥削性降低引入表格扑克领域。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret Minimization (MCCFR) repeatedly allocates chance outcomes while its strategy evolves, yet standard sampling draws those outcomes independently on every visit. We introduce Correlated Chance Sampling MCCFR (CCS-MCCFR), a drop-in replacement that assigns each concrete chance node a persistent randomized Weyl stream and maps its phases through the node's chance distribution. Each fixed-index draw has the correct marginal law, while the first $N$ draws consumed during $N$ visits to one concrete node achieve deterministic local frequency error $O(\!\log(N+1)/N)$, compared with the $O(N^{-1/2})$ expected scale of i.i.d. frequencies. We further establish unbiasedness along fixed strategy trajectories, isolate adaptive phase selection through a conditional scalar bound, and show that a per-traversal reset variant retains the standard $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ External Sampling guarantee. In paired experiments, CCS-MCCFR reduces final exploitability by 19.05\% to 34.01\% across Kuhn poker and four Leduc poker configurations, with every paired-bootstrap confidence interval above zero, and by a significant 4.27\% on Goofspiel-4. The gain survives to 3M Leduc node touches and combines with Linear CFR to reach the lowest measured exploitability. The sampler introduces no new hyperparameters and no measurable time overhead, so CCS-MCCFR turns a one-line change to the chance sampler into explicit local guarantees and large exploitability reductions across tabular poker.

</details>


### [158] [The Computable but Not Learnable Information-Value-Free Equilibria and Regulation of Algorithmic Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27128)
*Jason D. Hartline,Chang Wang,Chenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类称为"信息价值无关"的相关均衡虽然可以高效离线计算，但无法被广泛的学习算法在线学习，这与传统均衡概念中离线计算与在线学习难度相当的情况形成鲜明对比。该结果意味着学习博弈中的相关均衡无法避免产生有价值的信息，对算法合谋监管具有重要政策含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息价值无关的相关均衡（information-value-free equilibrium）的可学习性，揭示离线计算与在线学习之间存在的根本性分离，并为算法合谋监管提供理论依据。

Method: 理论证明：通过构造反例和复杂度论证，证明信息价值无关的相关均衡虽然可高效离线计算，但无法被一类广泛的学习算法（仅使用自身收益的完整反馈）在线学习到。

Result: 信息价值无关的相关均衡存在离线计算与在线学习的分离：可高效计算但不可学习。这一分离导致学习算法无法避免产生有价值的信息，且对广泛学习算法而言，时间平均收敛到纳什均衡存在信息论意义上的不可能性。

Conclusion: 在理性化学习（rationalizable learning）下，有价值且隐性的信息交换是不可避免的，因此传统反垄断监管与理性化学习不相容。

Abstract: A correlated equilibrium is information-value-free if every player has an action that yields the same payoff as their equilibrium strategy when all other players follow their respective equilibrium strategies. An equilibrium is learnable if the empirical history of play generated by certain learning algorithms using only full feedback on each player's own payoff converges to it.
  Our main result is that although an information-value-free equilibrium can be computed efficiently offline, it is not learnable by a broad class of learning algorithms. This separation stands in sharp contrast to canonical equilibrium concepts, where offline computation and online learning typically have comparable difficulty.
  In information-economics terms, our results imply that learning correlated equilibria in games cannot avoid generating valuable information, which has implications for current debates on the regulation of algorithmic collusion: Valuable and implicit information exchange is unavoidable under rationalizable learning, and traditional antitrust regulation against such exchange is therefore incompatible with rationalizable learning. Our results also imply an informational impossibility result for time-average convergence to a Nash equilibrium by a broad class of learning algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针和均值消融实验，揭示了强化学习（RL）训练的大规模推理模型相较于监督微调（SFT）模型，在数学推理任务上表现更优的神经机制：RL模型形成更线性可分的表征和层级化关键层结构，而SFT模型则均匀分布重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练的推理模型在数学推理任务上显著优于SFT模型，但其背后的神经机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究RL模型实现这种性能优势的内部表征差异。

Method: 1) 在线性探针实验中，基于层间隐藏状态训练分类器，预测答案正确性，比较RL与SFT模型的准确率差异。2) 在均值消融实验中，逐层移除模型组件，评估各层对最终性能的相对重要性。3) 通过重复采样分析token数量变异性，衡量自适应计算分配。

Result: 1) RL模型在预测答案正确性上准确率更高，表征更线性可分。2) RL模型呈现层级化架构，深层逐渐变得更关键；SFT模型则均匀分布重要性。3) token分配变异性因模型而异，部分RL模型比SFT变异性更高，部分则一致，说明这可能更多取决于整体训练流程而非RL与SFT本身。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重构了模型在推理问题上的表征和计算架构，形成更结构化、层级化的内部表示。token分配变异性可能反映on-policy推理的扩散程度，有助于识别模型是否具有稳定策略或处于非确定性的欠定行为状态。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [160] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用社交推理游戏《Werewolf》评估LLM多智能体系统中目标错位的框架。通过修改单个智能体的目标（同时保留其角色），分析了四个模型家族、四种角色和三种目标设定下的内部推理、公共廉价谈话行为及游戏结果。结果表明，目标错位会损害对抗性环境中的集体决策，且这种影响在公共行为中难以察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统越来越多地部署在混合动机环境中，智能体在不对称信息和策略性欺骗下运作，目标与集体目标不一致（目标错位）成为一个核心问题，亟需有效的评估框架来量化这种错位的影响。

Method: 以社交推理游戏《Werewolf》为测试平台，修改单个智能体的目标（同时保留其角色分配），跨四个LLM模型家族、四种玩家角色和三种目标设定进行实验。采用双重分析：一是智能体的内部推理（推理链），二是公共廉价谈话行为（无成本、非约束性、不直接影响效用的通信），并结合游戏结果分析。

Result: 目标错位会损害对抗性环境中的游戏结果，这种效应在不对称信息和专业化角色下更为加剧。被篡改的智能体会发展出与目标相关的独特推理策略，但这些适应性调整在其公共行为中几乎不可见。即使细微的目标错位也会深刻影响集体决策。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM多智能体系统中目标错位的隐蔽性和破坏性，强调了在LLM多智能体系统中需要有效的缓解策略来应对目标错位问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles. While compromised agents consistently develop distinct objective-dependent reasoning strategies, these adaptations remain largely invisible in their public behavior. More broadly, our findings suggest that even subtle objective misalignment can profoundly affect collective decision-making, highlighting the need for effective mitigation strategies for LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [161] [ClinLens: Towards Long-Horizon Coding Agents for Longitudinal Multimodal Clinical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26155)
*Yuan Zhu,Ethan B. Liu,Frank Nie,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLINLENS是一个包含200个可执行任务的基准测试，覆盖MIMIC资源中的结构化电子健康记录、临床笔记、心电图、胸片和超声心动图。采用4×5分类法（4种患者时间范围×5种分析能力），通过程序优先的反向合成方法进行评估。最强配置在126个任务上仅达56.3%的STRICTPASS（尽管执行成功率达100%），而对比系统最高仅2.9%，揭示了可运行提交与正确临床分析之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将医学问答、结构化表格推理和通用科学知识库分离处理，缺乏能够评估临床数据科学智能体处理异构纵向记录并生成可审计分析能力的统一基准。

Method: 构建CLINLENS基准：包含200个可执行任务，覆盖5个链接的MIMIC资源（结构化EHR、笔记、ECG、胸片、超声心动图），采用4×5分类法（4种患者时间范围×5种分析能力），通过程序优先反向合成方法生成任务，并使用评估器私有的参考工作流验证工件、队列语义、时间语义和最终答案。

Result: 在126个任务的固定套件上，24种标准化模型-脚手架配置中最强的达到了56.3%的范围宏观STRICTPASS（执行成功率为100%）。一个单独配置的编码智能体解决了126个任务中的83个，而5个适配GPT-4o-mini的生物医学系统最高仅达2.9%的范围宏观STRICTPASS。

Conclusion: 可运行提交与正确临床分析之间存在显著差距，当前最强的通用模型-脚手架配置在临床数据分析任务上表现有限，而专用生物医学系统表现更差，表明该领域需要进一步的研究与改进。

Abstract: Clinical data-science agents must transform heterogeneous longitudinal records into auditable analyses, yet existing benchmarks largely isolate medical question answering, structured-table reasoning, or generic scientific repositories. We introduce CLINLENS, a benchmark of 200 executable tasks over five linked MIMIC resources spanning structured electronic health records, notes, electrocardiograms, chest radiographs, and echocardiograms. A 4 x 5 taxonomy crosses four patient-time scopes with five analysis capabilities. Program-first reverse synthesis pairs each bounded semi-raw package with an evaluator-private reference workflow and checks required artifacts, cohort and temporal semantics, and the final answer. On a fixed 126-task suite, the strongest of 24 standardized model-scaffold configurations achieves 56.3% scope-macro STRICTPASS despite 100% EXECSUCCESS. For reference, a separately configured coding agent solves 83 of 126 tasks, while five biomedical systems adapted to GPT-4o-mini reach at most 2.9% scope-macro STRICTPASS. These results expose a substantial gap between runnable submissions and correct clinical analyses.

</details>


### [162] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基准测试结果到实际应用主张的推理中存在“可投射性”问题：即使相邻推理步骤各自有效，步骤之间的衔接可能因端点错位、假设不一致或依赖关系未被传递而导致整体论证无效。核心贡献是非组合性原则与可投射性审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有效度中心方法要求每一步都有证据支持，但未解决相邻步骤间衔接是否合理的问题——即使相邻推理链环各自成立，链条整体未必成立，因为目标与来源可能错位、系统/人群/结果/条件在接口处发生变化、共享数据或模型谱系使看似独立的支持实际上相互依赖。

Method: 引入Goodman的“竞争性扩展”问题与基于论证的效度框架，提出非组合性原则（相邻投射的合成仅在端点与假设对齐、依赖与不确定性被传递时才成立）；通过法律研究案例和重分析与模拟进行验证。

Result: 法律研究案例表明基准证据与部署研究各自成立但保持平行（无法有效衔接）；重分析与模拟显示聚合稳定性会抹消后续投射所需的关键区分。

Conclusion: 提出可投射性审计方法，用于诊断从基准到实际使用论证中的无效衔接问题。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


### [163] [GuideSkill: Evolving Executable LLM Agent Skills for Guideline-Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26160)
*Lang Cao,Yuhao Shen,Tianyang Luo,Simo Du,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuideSkill是一种将临床指南中的诊断标准编译为可执行函数的外部推理层，通过初始化和进化两种方式生成诊断技能，显著提升了LLM的疾病诊断准确性，并且无需更新骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM系统在处理临床实践指南时，通常只是检索指南文本或通过训练吸收知识，而非真正执行指南中的规则化诊断标准，导致诊断准确性受限。

Method: 提出GuideSkill外部推理层，将疾病特异性标准编译为返回序数诊断支持分数的可执行函数。GuideSkill-Zero从指南初始化，GuideSkill-Evo利用病例-诊断对来完善已有技能并补充缺失诊断。推理时，LLM提出鉴别诊断，将每个匹配技能所需的特征进行基础化，并将技能执行分数与LLM排序融合。

Result: 在四个基准和四个骨干模型上，GuideSkill-Zero相比指南RAG平均提升13.45%的宏平均准确率。GuideSkill-Evo在每个骨干模型上均取得最高宏平均，相比直接推理相对提升18.49%，金标技能覆盖率从56.5%提升至99.5%。在Qwen3.5-9B上，无需更新骨干模型即超越最强参数更新基线11.16%。专家评估表明技能在临床上是合理且可接受的。

Conclusion: 可执行技能是一种模型无关的机制，能够有效结合指南推导的程序与病例推导的诊断模式，提升LLM诊断性能。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode diagnostic criteria, but LLM systems typically retrieve guideline text or absorb it through training rather than execute its rules. We introduce GuideSkill, an external reasoning layer that compiles disease-specific criteria into executable functions returning ordinal diagnostic-support scores. GuideSkill-Zero is initialized from guidelines, while GuideSkill-Evo uses case--diagnosis pairs to refine covered skills and add missing diagnoses. At inference, an LLM proposes a differential diagnosis, grounds the features required by each matched skill, and fuses its ranking with the executed skill scores. Across four benchmarks and four backbones, GuideSkill-Zero improves macro-average accuracy over guideline RAG by 13.45% on average. GuideSkill-Evo achieves the highest macro-average for every backbone, improves over direct inference by 18.49% relatively, and increases gold-label skill coverage from 56.5% to 99.5%. On Qwen3.5-9B, it also exceeds the strongest parameter-update baseline by 11.16% without updating the backbone. Expert evaluation further indicates that GuideSkill produces clinically sound and broadly acceptable skills, suggesting that its initialized and evolved rules are reliable and practically meaningful. These results support executable skills as a model-agnostic mechanism for combining guideline-derived procedures with case-derived diagnostic patterns.

</details>


### [164] [GoGoTB: Agentic RTL Verification with Specification-Grounded Coverage Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26181)
*Xin Xin,Jincheng Lou,Junhui Li,Jinglin Yan,Panda Xiao,Di Wu,Haixiao Li,Weicong Lu,Weijian Fan,Xinyu Qu,Yuxiang Zhao,Min Yu,Zhixiong Di,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoGoTB是一个基于智能体（agentic）的端到端IC功能验证框架，通过三层子系统（执行控制层、可进化知识系统、规格驱动的覆盖率闭合）实现无需人工干预的验证环境生成与高覆盖率，在8个RTL设计上达到100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.2%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%，超越了所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的验证方法通过独立单轮调用生成各组件，缺乏共享上下文，导致接口不匹配未被检测、报告覆盖率与规格要求脱节，而功能验证占据IC前端工程主要工作量且遗漏bug可能导致昂贵的重新流片。

Method: GoGoTB提出agentic框架，包含三个子系统：(1) 执行控制层，在每个工具和阶段边界将确定性执行与LLM推理分离；(2) 可进化知识系统，按需调度方法论和设计特定知识；(3) 规格驱动的覆盖率框架，将每个覆盖率bin锚定到命名的规格行为，使残留缺口具有可诊断根本原因和针对性修复方案。

Result: 在8个RTL设计上无需任何人工干预，实现100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.2%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%。先前工作未能在相同基准上成功生成完整验证环境或达到有意义的覆盖率。

Conclusion: GoGoTB证明了agentic框架可以有效解决LLM在IC功能验证中的上下文缺失和覆盖率脱节问题，实现了端到端验证闭环，为自动化功能验证提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Functional verification dominates integrated circuit (IC) front-end engineering effort, and a single missed bug that escapes to silicon can trigger a costly respin. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate this process, yet existing LLM-based approaches generate each component through independent single-turn calls with no shared context, leaving interface mismatches undetected and reported coverage disconnected from specification requirements. To address these challenges, we present GoGoTB, an agentic framework that achieves end-to-end verification closure through three subsystems: an agentic execution control layer, an evolvable knowledge system, and specification-grounded coverage closure. The execution control layer separates deterministic enforcement from LLM reasoning at every tool and stage boundary. The knowledge system dispatches methodology and design-specific expertise on demand. The coverage framework anchors every bin to a named specification behavior so that each residual gap has a diagnosable root cause and a targeted remedy. Tested on 8 register transfer level (RTL) designs without any human intervention, GoGoTB achieves 100\% environment generation success and averages 98.4\% line, 97.2\% branch, 97.0\% toggle, and 83.2\% functional coverage. No prior work successfully generates a complete verification environment or achieves meaningful coverage on the same benchmarks.

</details>


### [165] [CG-World: A Large-Scale World-State Dataset and Protocol for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26452)
*Yiming Cai,Fangjie Yu,Meiqing Yu,Ziyue Shi,Pengfei Yuan,Yong Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CG-World是一个从工业计算机图形学管线导出的百万级世界状态数据集，显式记录了多模态语义、空间结构、骨骼控制器状态、运动曲线、物理缓存、接触事件及多通道渲染等中间状态，并定义了事实轨迹、观测干预、动作干预、机制干预和严格反事实分支的谱系，为世界模型、物理AI和具身智能提供结构化监督和反事实推理支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频、机器人和仿真数据集通常只捕捉状态、动作、事件和观测的部分结构，缺乏完整联合动态的标注，限制了世界模型学习状态-观测-事件之间的因果关系和反事实推理能力。

Method: 从工业CG生产管线中提取包含约85万个1-5秒时齐片段的数据集，将潜在状态、观测、关系、事件和分支元数据组织为统一时空样本；定义涵盖事实轨迹、观测/动作/机制干预及严格反事实分支的谱系，并显式记录干预目标、不变性和替代结果。

Result: 在几何条件视频生成、动作预测和闭环视觉-语言-动作策略迁移任务上验证了数据集的有效性，表明其能为可控生成、动作建模和具身策略迁移提供可复用的结构化监督。

Conclusion: CG-World为世界模型、物理AI和具身智能提供了统一的数据基础设施，未来将通过持续数据收集和社区协作扩展。

Abstract: World models must learn the joint dynamics of states, actions, events, and observations, yet existing video, robotics, and simulation datasets usually capture only part of this structure. We introduce CG-World, a large-scale world-state dataset and protocol derived from industrial computer graphics production pipelines. CG-World explicitly records intermediate states, including multimodal semantics, spatial structure, skeletal and controller states, motion curves, camera and lighting parameters, physics caches, contact events, and multi-pass renderings. CG-World v1 contains approximately 850,000 temporally aligned segments of 1-5 seconds. It separates latent states, observations, relations, events, and branch metadata, and organizes them into unified spatiotemporal samples. To support intervention learning and counterfactual reasoning, CG-World defines a branch lineage covering factual trajectories, observation interventions, action interventions, mechanism interventions, and strict counterfactual branches, with intervention targets, invariants, and alternative outcomes explicitly recorded. We evaluate the dataset on geometry-conditioned video generation, action prediction, and closed-loop vision-language-action policy transfer. Results show that CG-World provides reusable structured supervision for controlled generation, action modeling, and embodied policy transfer. We plan to expand CG-World through continued data collection and community collaboration toward a shared data infrastructure for world models, Physical AI, and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [166] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前语言模型评估中多信号平均聚合会导致“信任膨胀”，主张将评估得分视为具有形式性、范围性和有效期三种属性的认知主张，并基于多种理论传统提出最弱链聚合作为保守评估方法，通过在HELM排行榜上的实验证明均值聚合与最弱链聚合的排名结果完全不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型评估方法将自动化指标、LLM-as-judge评分、人工评估和基准测试结果等多种信号通过平均方式聚合，导致评估置信度远超最弱信号的可靠性，产生“信任膨胀”现象，亟需更严谨的评估框架。

Method: 论文从形式性、范围性和有效期三个属性重新定义评估得分，借鉴思维链分析、可能性逻辑和代数理论等研究传统，建立以悲观参数控制的参数化算子族，提出最弱链聚合作为保守端点，并在HELM排行榜的54个前沿模型、10个场景上进行实证对比。

Result: 在HELM排行榜上，均值聚合与最弱链聚合排名前五的模型完全不重叠，表明聚合方式对评估结果有根本性影响，均值聚合掩盖了模型在不同维度上的薄弱环节。

Conclusion: 评估结果应附带显式元数据（形式性层级、范围声明和过期日期）以提高认知透明度，最弱链聚合作为保守评估方法能更真实反映模型能力，避免信任膨胀。

Abstract: Evaluation methodologies for language models increasingly combine multiple signals, from automated metrics and LLM-as-judge ratings to human assessments and benchmark suite results. When these signals are aggregated via averaging, evaluation confidence can then substantially exceed the reliability of the weakest signal: a phenomenon we call trust inflation in evaluation. We argue that evaluation scores should be treated as epistemic claims with three properties: formality (human evaluation provides stronger evidence than an automated metric), scope (a benchmark result applies to the tested distribution, not universally), and validity windows (benchmark results expire as contamination accumulates and distributions shift). Several converging research traditions (chain-of-thought analysis, possibilistic logic, and algebraic theory) establish weakest-link aggregation as the conservative endpoint of a parameterized operator family controlled by a single pessimism parameter. Drawing on those traditions, and on concrete lessons from building an evaluation harness for agentic AI, we propose that evaluation results carry explicit metadata (formality tier, scope declaration, and expiration date) to make their epistemic status transparent. We illustrate the cost of mean aggregation on the public HELM leaderboard: across 54 frontier models on ten scenarios, the top-five models ranked by mean score and by weakest-link are completely disjoint.

</details>


### [167] [TraceCoder: Explainable and Auditable Code Generation with Position-Key Snippet Versioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26307)
*Rwaida Alssadi,Muntaser Syed,Balaji Kasula,Lamine Deen,Majed Alotaibi,Mohammed Alghamdi,Tyler Ton,Ali Alqarni,Marius Silaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceCoder系统，通过关系型片段历史模式、浏览器可视化和竞争性位置键索引机制，使LLM代码生成过程可审计、可追溯、可重放，在30个算法任务上实现平均30%的修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的代码生成智能体将代码视为黑盒输出，每行代码背后的推理过程不透明，通过基准驱动的修复形成的代码演化过程转瞬即逝，事后审计无法进行。

Method: (i) 关系型片段历史模式：记录每次修复事件的基准引用、轮次编号、失败文本和LLM解释，支持完整溯源查询；(ii) 基于浏览器的可视化工具：将历史渲染为热力图和悬停注释的源代码；(iii) 竞争性分数位置键索引方案：使用树节点分隔符分配稳定、字典序有序的标识符，实现细粒度跟踪而不干扰周围代码。

Result: 在30个算法编程任务上评估，10个任务耗尽6轮迭代预算。平均修复率（Chg%）达30%，三个片段中有十个携带可追溯的修复事件行，而使用Gemini 2.0 Flash单一提供商在20个任务子集上仅为21%。

Conclusion: TraceCoder使自动化代码生成的内部"叙事"变得可审计和可重放，这一特性对于生产部署中的信任和问责至关重要。

Abstract: Contemporary LLM-based coding agents produce code as black-box outputs: the rationale behind each line is hidden, the evolution of the code through benchmark-driven repair is ephemeral, and post-hoc auditing is impossible. We present a code generation concept that addresses these shortcomings through three complementary mechanisms: (i) a relational snippet-history schema that records, per repair event, the benchmark reference, round number, failure text, and LLM explanation, enabling full provenance queries; (ii) a browser-based visualisation tool that renders this history as heat-mapped, hover-annotated source code; and (iii) a competitive fractional position-key indexing scheme with tree-node delimiters that assigns stable, lexicographically-ordered identifiers to each code snippet, enabling fine-grained tracking without disrupting surrounding lines. We evaluate TraceCoder on 30 algorithmic programming tasks spanning string processing, mathematical computation, and data-structure manipulation, across two provider configurations. Of these, 10 exhaust the 6-iteration budget on tasks with subtle edge-case behaviour. Mean Chg% reaches 30%, three in ten code snippets carry a traceable repair-event row, compared to 21% when using Gemini 2.0 Flash as sole provider on a 20-task subset. Three detailed case studies demonstrate how the system explains which specific benchmark failures shaped each line of the final program. The proposed mechanism makes the internal "narrative" of automated code generation auditable and replayable, a property essential for trust and accountability in production deployments.

</details>


### [168] [Linguistic Monoculture in LLM-Assisted Language Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27134)
*Suhas Thejaswi,Juhi Kulshreshta,Lutz Oettershagen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个数学框架，研究大语言模型（LLMs）作为写作辅助工具时，如何通过共享模型、递归反馈和个性化模型三种机制，导致作者群体语言特征趋于同质化（即“语言单一文化”），并分析了其中的社会外部性与效率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs被广泛用于文本起草、修订和润色，虽然能提高清晰度，但共享模型的广泛使用可能降低群体层面的语言变异，导致语言单一文化。作者希望通过数学建模来理解这一现象的机制和后果。

Method: 作者将作者和LLMs表示为语言特征上的分布，通过三个机制（固定共享模型、递归更新共享模型、个性化模型）建模共同演化，并内生化作者的遵从性选择，构建包含清晰度、可读性、流畅度与独特风格之间权衡的效用模型。

Result: 1）共享模型驱使作者趋同；2）递归反馈改变共同规范但不改变配对离散度；3）个性化可保留多样的作者-模型平衡态；4）理性作者可能比社会最优更加遵从，因为他们没有内化自身独特性对他人的价值，产生负外部性；5）单一文化的代价在固定实例中有限，但当独特性主导真实性时可能无界增长。

Conclusion: LLM辅助写作可能导致语言多样性下降，个性化模型可以缓解这一问题，但个体理性与社会最优之间存在差距，需要关注语言单一文化的社会代价。

Abstract: Writing and communication are increasingly mediated by large language models (LLMs) that are being used to draft, revise and polish text. Although such assistance can improve clarity and help authors meet institutional expectations, widespread reliance on shared models may reduce population-level variation in linguistic form, a phenomenon we refer to as linguistic monoculture. We develop a mathematical framework in which authors and LLMs are represented as distributions over linguistic features and coevolve through repeated interaction. We analyze three interaction mechanisms: a shared model with a fixed linguistic distribution, a shared model recursively updated from author outputs, and personalized models updated through author-specific and population-level feedback. We characterize the resulting equilibria and convergence rates, showing that, shared models can drive authors toward a common norm, recursive feedback relocates the shared norm without altering pairwise spread under common conformity, and personalization can preserve a family of distinct author-model equilibria with nonzero linguistic diversity. We then endogenize conformity as a strategic choice trading off private benefits from clarity, legibility, and perceived fluency against distinctive style. Within this utility model, individually rational authors may conform more than is socially optimal because they do not internalize the value their distinctiveness provides to others, creating a negative externality and a price of monoculture that is finite for each fixed instance but can grow without bound when distinctiveness dominates authenticity. Synthetic simulations illustrate how fixed shared assistance, recursive feedback, and personalization produce different long-run diversity outcomes.

</details>


### [169] [Exploring Structures in Physics Problems: Can AI Agents Discover Statistical Mechanical Mappings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26367)
*Wanyu Zhao,Wanbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StatMechBench-v0基准，用于评估基于LLM的智能体能否从原始配分函数中自动发现统计力学映射（如Ising模型中的转移矩阵、规范可消无序、平面/Pfaffian结构）。通过propose-verify-revise框架实验发现，数值反馈虽能帮助智能体修复代码并恢复正确的配分函数，但智能体可能在通过数值验证的同时错误识别可解类或低估计算复杂度，表明当前LLM推理存在局限，需要超越数值一致性验证的符号检查和结构不变性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论物理中的一项关键技能是将新问题转化为已知模型。作者希望探究LLM智能体能否从原始配分函数自动发现统计力学映射，从而评估AI在理论物理结构发现中的能力。

Method: 构建包含六类Ising问题的StatMechBench-v0基准（涵盖转移矩阵方法、规范可消无序、平面/Pfaffian结构），采用propose-verify-revise智能体框架，在多种LLM和问题表述下进行实验评估。

Result: 数值反馈常能帮助智能体修复代码并恢复正确的配分函数，但智能体可能在通过数值检查的同时误判底层可解类或低估计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 当前LLM推理存在局限，AI智能体的验证栈不能仅依赖数值一致性，还需引入符号检查和结构不变性等验证手段。

Abstract: An important skill in theoretical physics is to recognize when a new problem can be transformed into a known model. We study this skill as an AI-agent task: can LLM-based agents discover statistical mechanical mappings from a raw partition function to a tractable representation? To probe this question, we introduce StatMechBench-v0, a benchmark of six Ising-type problems covering transfer-matrix methods, gauge-removable disorder, and planar/Pfaffian structure. We evaluate a simple propose-verify-revise agent across multiple LLMs and problem phrasings. The results show that numerical feedback often helps agents repair code and recover correct partition functions. However, agents can also pass the numerical checks while misidentifying the underlying tractable class or understating computational complexity. This both reveals limitations in current LLM reasoning and calls for a verification stack that goes beyond numerical agreement, incorporating, for example, symbolic checks and structural invariants. Our study provides an early evaluation and design directions for AI agents aimed at structural discovery in theoretical physics.

</details>


### [170] [CaM-Wolf: Causal-Aware Multimodal Agents for Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26393)
*Zheng Zhang,Nanjie Yao,Jiarui He,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CaM-Wolf，首个集成多模态感知与生成的社会推理游戏AI智能体，通过因果感知推理和动画化身，在狼人杀游戏中实现更优表现和人机交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会推理游戏AI主要基于文本，忽视了多模态交互（如视觉信息）在人类社会互动中的基础性作用。

Method: CaM-Wolf包含三部分：1) 多模态感知模块，处理其他玩家的视频输入；2) 因果感知推理器，通过强化学习训练，建立可观察行为与隐藏角色之间的逻辑链；3) 动画化身，用于可视化呈现。

Result: 实验和用户研究表明，CaM-Wolf在智能体游戏表现和人类-AI交互质量上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作向创建能参与复杂社交动态的更类人AI智能体迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) such as Werewolf have become challenging testbeds for AI agents. These games require complex social skills such as reasoning, deception, and collaboration. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in SDG agents, current approaches are predominantly text-based, overlooking the multimodal nature that is fundamental to human social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce CaM-Wolf, the first SDG agent that integrates multimodal perception and generation. CaM-Wolf processes video inputs from other players, employs a causal-aware Reasoner trained via reinforcement learning to establish logical chains between observable behaviors and hidden roles, and presents itself through an animated avatar. Our experiments and user study show that CaM-Wolf achieves superior agent gameplay performance and enhances the quality of human-AI interaction. This work represents a significant advancement towards creating more human-like AI agents capable of participating in nuanced social dynamics. Our code is available at https://3dagentworld.github.io/avatar_wolf.

</details>


### [171] [MultivationBench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Sequential Motivation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26465)
*Kawai Chung,Chunkit Chan,Yauwai Yim,Yuxuan Liu,Haochen Shi,Weiqi Wang,Qing Zong,Tianshi Zheng,Yixuan Fu,Kai Chung Wong,Hao Liang,Yifan Gao,Xi Yang,Janet Hui-wen Hsiao,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MultivationBench是一个评估多模态大模型在故事驱动的视觉叙事中进行序列动机推理能力的基准。基于马斯洛需求层次和Reiss基本欲望理论框架，要求模型整合累积的多模态上下文来推断演变的动机。测试结果显示所有模型在跨序列上下文保持一致的动机推理方面都存在困难，揭示了静态识别能力与动态推理之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注静态文本或孤立视觉快照，无法反映真实世界行为驱动因素的累积特性，多模态大模型的序列动机推理能力尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 构建MultivationBench基准，基于马斯洛需求层次和Reiss基本欲望等心理学框架，通过故事驱动的视觉叙事要求模型整合累积多模态上下文来推断演变的动机。

Result: 所有测试模型在跨序列上下文中保持一致的动机推理方面均表现困难，表明静态识别能力与类人社会理解所需的动态推理之间存在关键脱节。

Conclusion: MultivationBench对现有模型构成显著挑战，揭示了多模态大模型在动态动机推理能力上的不足。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have sparked significant interest due to their potential for social intelligence; however, their ability to perform sequential motivation reasoning remains insufficiently studied. Existing evaluations predominantly examine static text or isolated visual snapshots, which do not reflect the cumulative nature of real-world behavioral drivers. To address this gap, we introduce MultivationBench, a benchmark designed to rigorously evaluate multimodal motivation reasoning within story-driven visual narratives. The benchmark builds upon established psychological frameworks - Maslow's hierarchy and Reiss's basic desires - and requires models to integrate accumulated multimodal context to infer evolving motivations. Results indicate that MultivationBench presents a significant challenge: all tested models struggle to maintain consistent motivation reasoning across sequential contexts, revealing a critical disconnect between static recognition capabilities and the dynamic reasoning essential for human-like social understanding.

</details>


### [172] [EvoPINN: Agentic Discovery of Executable Algorithms for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26490)
*Peng Yin,Kai Li,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoPINN是一个基于LLM智能体的自动化框架，将PINN开发从人工手动设计转变为执行驱动的算法发现过程，通过模块化搜索空间、结构验证和预算匹配评估，自动发现性能更优的PDE专用学习算法，并自主发明了SLRC-PINN全新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN的性能高度依赖人工试错式的神经网络表示、损失函数设计和优化策略调整，而LLM直接生成代码的方式在严格的科学计算约束下常产生数学无效或不稳定的解，亟需一种兼顾自动化与科学严谨性的框架。

Method: EvoPINN将神经表示与训练程序解耦，构建模块化搜索空间；利用LLM智能体基于记忆条件迭代提出程序化修改；对所有候选方案执行严格的结构验证和预算匹配的PDE评估，确保科学有效性。

Result: 在振荡型、椭圆型、耗散型和非线性输运等多种PDE场景下，EvoPINN发现的PDE专用学习算法相比基线显著降低了相对L2误差；并自主发明了SLRC-PINN架构，在参数严格匹配下性能优势依然保持。

Conclusion: EvoPINN验证了执行驱动型智能体能够自主发现真正新颖的科学计算机制，为科学计算中的自动算法发现开辟了新途径。
